独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 37621548015 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

重要易用性改进上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审：待确认（suspected）
F6 · 页面在异步保存后关闭 overlay 缺少受支持路径，Agent 自建 RouteOverlayBridge

应用模板 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/overlay.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    overlay.md 明确 useRouteOverlay 只能在 overlay 内部调用，并给出把 close 部分拆成子组件的正确写法；但如果关闭必须发生在页面组件的异步保存之后（无子组件可调用 hook），模板只提供会抛错的 hook。冻结应用首版直接在页面调用 hook，verify-2 中所有自定义表单/动作路由页面报 “Route ... could not be loaded”，repair-2 新增 RouteOverlayBridge/useRouteOverlayRef 才通过 verify-3。

    实际影响
    常见“保存成功后关闭弹窗”的表单场景需要自建未文档化的 ref 桥接，首次实现失败并触发一轮返工。
    建议改法
    在模板/指引中提供一个受支持的页面级关闭范式（如 bridge 组件或页面可见的 controls），并在 overlay.md 记录；或让 useRouteOverlay 在页面层返回可用的 controls。
    诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
    触发条件
    弹窗表单保存成功后需要由页面组件关闭当前 overlay。
    应有能力 / 行为
    存在受支持且在 Skill 中记录的方式，让页面在异步保存后安全关闭 overlay。
    实际观察
    页面在 overlay 外调用 useRouteOverlay 抛错，全部自定义表单/动作路由无法加载；需自建 RouteOverlayBridge 存 ref 后关闭。
    应用绕行与代价
    新增 RouteOverlayBridge + useRouteOverlayRef，把 controls 抄入 ref，保存后 ref.current?.close()。
    改进后的验收标准
    overlay.md/模板给出页面级异步关闭范式后，同类弹窗表单无需自建桥接即可通过验收。
    评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
    app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/overlay.md:120–149E24

    指引：useRouteOverlay 只能在 overlay 内部调用；页面只渲染 RouteDialog，需要 close 的部分拆成子组件。

    `useRouteOverlay()` returns `{ close, isClosing }`:

- `close(): Promise<void>`: closes the current overlay layer. Calling it again while a close is in progress returns the same Promise.
- `isClosing`: `true` from the `close()` call until navigation finishes (including the wait for `beforeClose`); use it to disable buttons such as "Cancel".

**Call it only in a component inside the overlay**, including a component passed as `footer`. The context comes from `RouteDialog`/`RouteDrawer`, and the page component that renders the overlay is outside it:

```tsx
// Wrong: NewProjectPage renders the RouteDialog, so it is itself outside that RouteDialog.
export default function NewProjectPage(): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  // Throws when there is no overlay outside; when another overlay is outside, it gets that outer close and closes the wrong layer.
  const { close } = useRouteOverlay();
  return (
    <RouteDialog
      title={t('projects.create.title')}
      footer={
        <Button variant='outline' onClick={() => void close()}>
          {t('actions.cancel')}
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {/* … */}
    </RouteDialog>
  );
}
```

- With no overlay outside, it throws `useRouteOverlay must be used inside RouteDialog or RouteDrawer`. When nested, it does not throw; instead it silently gets the parent overlay's `close` and closes the wrong layer. Context is looked up through the parent-child relationships of React components, regardless of the source file or the DOM location a portal renders into.
- The right way: the page component only renders `RouteDialog`; split the parts that need to close into separate components (for example, `NewProjectBody` and `NewProjectFooter`) and render them as JSX (`<NewProjectFooter />`, not the function call `NewProjectFooter()`; otherwise the hook does not run inside the overlay).
    app/client/components/use-route-overlay.ts:20–44E30

    被修改的模板组件：useRouteOverlay 在 overlay 外抛错，并新增 useRouteOverlayRef 以承接页面级异步关闭。

    /**
 * Closes the enclosing `RouteDialog` or `RouteDrawer`. Call it from a component rendered inside the overlay — its
 * body or its footer — never from the page that returns the overlay, which sits outside the provider.
 */
export function useRouteOverlay(): RouteOverlayContextValue {
  const value = useContext(RouteOverlayContext);
  if (!value) {
    throw new Error(
      'useRouteOverlay must be used inside RouteDialog or RouteDrawer',
    );
  }
  return value;
}

/**
 * Captures the controls of the overlay a page renders.
 *
 * The page component that returns `RouteDialog`/`RouteDrawer` sits outside the overlay's context, so it cannot call
 * `useRouteOverlay()` directly. It renders `<RouteOverlayBridge controlsRef={ref} />` among the overlay's children;
 * the bridge captures the controls, and the page closes the overlay through `ref.current?.close()` after an async
 * save, when no descendant is left to do it.
 */
export function useRouteOverlayRef(): RefObject<RouteOverlayContextValue | null> {
  return useRef<RouteOverlayContextValue | null>(null);
}
    app/client/components/route-overlay-bridge.tsx:1–29E31

    应用新增 RouteOverlayBridge：渲染为空，把 overlay controls 抄进 ref，供页面保存后关闭。

    import { useLayoutEffect, type ReactElement, type RefObject } from 'react';

import {
  useRouteOverlay,
  type RouteOverlayContextValue,
} from './use-route-overlay.js';

export interface RouteOverlayBridgeProps {
  readonly controlsRef: RefObject<RouteOverlayContextValue | null>;
}

/**
 * Renders nothing and copies the enclosing overlay's controls into `controlsRef`.
 *
 * It must be rendered as a child of `RouteDialog`/`RouteDrawer`. It exists so the page component that returns the
 * overlay can close it after a save, which is the one place that has to happen from outside a descendant.
 */
export function RouteOverlayBridge({
  controlsRef,
}: RouteOverlayBridgeProps): ReactElement | null {
  const controls = useRouteOverlay();
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    controlsRef.current = controls;
    return () => {
      controlsRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [controls, controlsRef]);
  return null;
}

    F4 · 授权 Skill 只文档化 composite，公共的 catalog 资源类型/grantBacked 无指引

    文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md · 原评审：未解决

    ⌄

    反馈证据核对

    定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

      反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

      authorization.md 要求业务操作检查 composite 并绑定 conditions.database 策略、不得用 collection 级或内存过滤替代；但 core 公共导出同时提供 ResourceTypeRegistry/ResourceItems/grantBacked 的 catalog 资源类型路径，Skill 中无任何示例或取舍说明。冻结应用正是选择该未文档化路径并自行实现记录范围过滤。

      实际影响
      Agent 面对非 CRUD 业务动作时在两条路径间缺少判断依据，容易绕开记录级策略，削弱可审计性。
      建议改法
      在 authorization.md / code-and-seeds.md 增补 catalog 资源类型的适用边界与最小示例，并明确何时必须回退到 composite + withPolicy。
      诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
      触发条件
      为接收采购单、整单领用、导入导出等非 CRUD 动作声明授权。
      应有能力 / 行为
      Skill 说明 composite 与 catalog 资源类型两条路径的适用条件，给出 grantBacked/catalog 用法，并说明记录范围该用 recordAccess/withPolicy。
      实际观察
      Skill 只推 composite + withPolicy 且禁止 collection 级/内存过滤；catalog 资源类型与 grantBacked 仅存在于包导出中，Agent 据此自建资源类型并在 service 内过滤 owner。
      应用绕行与代价
      应用声明 office catalog 资源类型 + grantBacked，记录可见范围由 service 的 readOwn 过滤承担。
      改进后的验收标准
      Skill 增补 catalog 路径说明与示例后，同类任务无需自创资源类型或 service 级过滤即可通过验收。
      评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
      app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md:21–23E17

      指引：业务操作检查 composite 并绑定 conditions.database 策略，不得用 collection 级或内存过滤替代；页面用 page authz。

      Declare reusable collection permissions and business operations, defined as composite resources, in a feature module. In `server/providers/`, resolve `authorizationToken`, then register collections (`authz.database.collections.add`), composites (`authz.compositeResources.define`), record access (`authz.recordAccess.define`), settings items (`authz.settings.add`) and, when the feature owns a team or department model, inherited subjects (`authz.subjects.add`, built as [an organisation dimension](organization.md)); add workspace subsections with `authz.ui.sections.add` and list each composite or settings item in one with `authz.ui.place`. Pages are not registered on the server; the client routes list them. Read the authorization Skill’s bundled `references/code-and-seeds.md` before choosing initialization: code declares capabilities, provider boot registers them, and seeds initialize the agreed business configuration for fresh installations. Use authorized services or controlled data changes for existing installations. Seeded permission sets, selected scopes, rules and assignments remain editable in the backend; seeds do not make them code-owned or protected. App features own composite declarations and initial business configuration, not root/member protection or other platform permission configuration. Never rewrite administrator configuration at boot. Normal seeds have a restricted database context, not the running authorization service. Keep schema changes in migrations and initial assignments in idempotent seeds or controlled provisioning. Do not create a plugin package for an application-owned feature.

Every `server/routes/` endpoint installs authentication and authorization. A business operation checks `{ resource: { type: 'composite', id }, action }` with the request context's `authorize` once and binds each returned `conditions.database` policy through `repository.withPolicy`. Do not replace it with collection-level authorization or an in-memory filter. Use `authz.database.policyFor` for collection CRUD and `authz.database.authorizeRepository` for simple business Repository routes, mapping each method to its composite action. The authorization Skill documents these choices and their contracts.
      packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/index.d.ts:7–9E12

      core 公共导出包含记录访问、catalog/record 资源类型与 grantBacked、composite 声明。

      export { RecordAccessBuilder, RecordAccessRegistry, defineRecordAccess, type RecordAccessContext, type RecordAccessDefinition, type RecordAccessReference, } from './record-access.js';
export { ResourceItems, ResourceTypeRegistry, grantBacked, type AuthorizationRuntimeContext, type GrantBackedOptions, type RegisteredResourceType, type ResourceAuthorize, type ResourceAuthorizeUnrestricted, type ResourceItem, type ResourceItemAction, type ResourceItemDefinition, type ResourceTypeAction, type ResourceTypeDefinition, } from './resource-types.js';
export { AuthorizationRouteRegistry, type AuthorizationRouteEntry, type AuthorizationRouteHandler, type AuthorizationRouteRequest, } from './routes.js';
      packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/resource-types.js:117–154E13

      ResourceTypeRegistry 校验 catalog/record 类型、动作唯一与保留的 composite 类型；未声明 authorize 时默认 grantBacked()。

      /**
 * Every resource type. A catalog type has items and validates the item and
 * action; a record type has none, validates only the action and leaves the
 * record id to its own judgement.
 */
export class ResourceTypeRegistry {
    registered = new Map();
    constructor() {
        handlers.set(this, new Map());
    }
    add(definition) {
        const type = definition.type;
        if (!type)
            throw new TypeError('A resource type needs a name');
        if (type === RESERVED_TYPE && !addingReserved)
            throw new Error(`Resource type ${RESERVED_TYPE} is reserved for composite resources; use authz.compositeResources.define`);
        if (this.registered.has(type))
            throw new Error(`Resource type already registered: ${type}`);
        const actions = definition.actions?.map((action) => typeof action === 'string' ? { name: action } : action);
        const names = actions?.map((action) => action.name) ?? [];
        if (names.some((name) => !name) || new Set(names).size !== names.length)
            throw new TypeError(`Resource type ${type} needs unique, nonempty actions`);
        const items = definition.items;
        if (!items && !actions?.length)
            throw new TypeError(`Resource type ${type} needs items or declared actions`);
        const declared = actions?.map(({ name, title }) => title === undefined ? { name } : { name, title });
        if (items) {
            if (itemBindings.has(items))
                throw new Error(`Resource items already belong to a type: ${type}`);
            const binding = {
                type,
                ...(declared ? { actions: declared } : {}),
            };
            for (const item of itemEntries.get(items)?.values() ?? [])
                validateItem(item, binding);
            itemBindings.set(items, binding);
        }
        const typeAuthorize = definition.authorize ?? grantBacked();
      app/server/office/authorization.ts:13–45E28

      应用把办公用品声明为自有 catalog 资源类型 office，并用 grantBacked 兜底，注释明确不采用 composite。

      /**
 * Office supplies is a catalog resource type of its own.
 *
 * It is deliberately not a composite or a database collection type. The
 * operations the business needs — receiving a purchase order, fulfilling a
 * requisition as one atomic whole, importing and exporting inventory — are not
 * per-record CRUD, so a grant list keyed by area and action describes them
 * directly, and an administrator reading Settings → Authorization sees exactly
 * the capabilities the feature checks. Composite data scopes would have to be
 * expanded and kept valid against evolving collection metadata, and the checks
 * would still live in the service: the extra machinery buys nothing here, so it
 * is not registered.
 */
export const OFFICE_RESOURCE_TYPE = 'office';

/** The translation namespace of this application's own strings. */
const NS = 'nb3-factory';

export interface OfficeAreaDefinition {
  /** The catalog item id, also the `resource.id` of every grant on that area. */
  readonly id: string;
  /** The item's title, translated by the client. */
  readonly titleKey: string;
  /** The actions the item exposes. */
  readonly actions: readonly string[];
}

/**
 * The areas, in the order the workspace catalog shows them, with the actions
 * each exposes. Adding an action here makes it grantable; the route and the
 * service must still check it.
 */
export const OFFICE_AREAS: readonly OfficeAreaDefinition[] = [

      ### [轻微][指引缺失] 授权 Skill 只文档化 composite，公共的 catalog 资源类型/grantBacked 无指引

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/authorization`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.11）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

authorization.md 要求业务操作检查 composite 并绑定 conditions.database 策略、不得用 collection 级或内存过滤替代；但 core 公共导出同时提供 ResourceTypeRegistry/ResourceItems/grantBacked 的 catalog 资源类型路径，Skill 中无任何示例或取舍说明。冻结应用正是选择该未文档化路径并自行实现记录范围过滤。

**影响**：Agent 面对非 CRUD 业务动作时在两条路径间缺少判断依据，容易绕开记录级策略，削弱可审计性。

**建议改动**：在 authorization.md / code-and-seeds.md 增补 catalog 资源类型的适用边界与最小示例，并明确何时必须回退到 composite + withPolicy。

**验收标准**：Skill 增补 catalog 路径说明与示例后，同类任务无需自创资源类型或 service 级过滤即可通过验收。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md:21–23` — 指引：业务操作检查 composite 并绑定 conditions.database 策略，不得用 collection 级或内存过滤替代；页面用 page authz。
- `packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/index.d.ts:7–9` — core 公共导出包含记录访问、catalog/record 资源类型与 grantBacked、composite 声明。
- `packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/resource-types.js:117–154` — ResourceTypeRegistry 校验 catalog/record 类型、动作唯一与保留的 composite 类型；未声明 authorize 时默认 grantBacked()。
- `app/server/office/authorization.ts:13–45` — 应用把办公用品声明为自有 catalog 资源类型 office，并用 grantBacked 兜底，注释明确不采用 composite。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 37621548015 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。

      Agent / 业务实现 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md · 原评审：未解决

      ⌄

      未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

      code-and-seeds.md 给出的种子形状是 id=permissionSet.key、encodeAuthorizationTitle(title)、transaction:true；冻结应用改为 crypto.randomUUID() 作 id、JSON.stringify({key,ns}) 作 title，且未显式开启事务。先查后插的幂等与 grants 形状仍正确。

      实际影响
      确定性 id 与统一 title 编解码被绕过，安装数据与管理员后续编辑的一致性较弱，也增加审计成本。
      建议改法
      按文档改用 permissionSet.key 作 id、encodeAuthorizationTitle，并显式 transaction:true。
      诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
      触发条件
      为三个角色写入初始 Permission Set 与分配。
      应有能力 / 行为
      使用文档化 helper 与确定性 id，种子可重跑且与运行时编解码一致。
      实际观察
      手写插入行、随机 UUID 与裸 JSON title，未显式事务。
      应用绕行与代价
      先按 key 查重再插入，功能上达到幂等。
      改进后的验收标准
      种子改用文档化 helper 后，权限工作区展示与管理员编辑仍保持一致。

      应用绕行与代价
      改用 agent-browser download（retro 记录）。
      改进后的验收标准

      本轮过程记录

      Repaired the office-supplies system across two rounds. Round 1 (repair-1) cleared the lint gate. Round 2 (repair-2) fixed every custom office form/action route failing to load: the page components that return RouteDialog/RouteDrawer called useRouteOverlay() outside the overlay context, so the hook threw and the route boundary rendered "could not be loaded". Added a ref bridge for those pages, fixed stale-state and !result close guards, and verified all routes plus the inventory CSV download.

      实现者未记录具体阻塞，不等于本轮没有问题。

      实现者补充建议

      来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

      建议 01 · 验收机制

      Verify file exports with a download-aware action

      可自动化

      The QA step clicked the Export link with a plain automation click, which the browser cancels ("Download was canceled"). The same unchanged link downloads correctly through `agent-browser download` and through a Playwright download event (failure=null), so the export path itself is sound.

      建议改动

      When a verification step expects a file, drive it with `agent-browser download <sel> <path>` or a Playwright download event instead of a plain click.

      来源：Agent · retro.json

      框架职责与业务边界

      E25

      需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

      以 RouteDialog/RouteDrawer 呈现的表单在保存/取消后正确关闭并返回父路由。需要绕行
      框架职责与业务边界
      框架提供 overlay 组件与 useRouteOverlay 上下文；应用编写页面与表单。
      推荐使用方式
      页面只渲染 overlay，把需要 close 的部分拆成子组件并在 overlay 内部调用 hook。
      Agent 实际使用

      应用模板
      E24 E30 E31

      框架职责与业务边界

      E5 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:138–142

      指引要求以 query/builder 做事务内数据库操作，历史 schema 必须自包含。

      Migration callbacks and seed `run` also receive `container`, a `ServiceResolver` exposing `has(token)`, `resolve(token)`, and `resolveIfCreated(token)`. Application task runners wrap the application container with a token-identity allowlist; only `idGeneratorToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/id-generator` is currently allowed. Use `container.resolve(idGeneratorToken).generateString()` for string IDs or `.generate()` for numeric IDs. Disallowed tokens return false from `has` and throw from both resolution methods. The wrapper reuses application singleton instances and exposes no registration methods. Startup and CLI share the application factory in `server/app.ts`, which registers `IdGeneratorProvider`; CLI database commands call the application factory discovered from `rootDir/server/app.ts` (or `.js` in compiled applications), register its providers without booting or starting them, and use its database manager and migration contributions. They dispose the application and runtime scope after execution, including failure and cancellation. Keep the factory free of startup side effects; CLI does not call `app.start()` or trigger autoRun. Services must therefore be usable after provider registration; do not depend on later boot/start hooks. Direct application and database runners accept a `container` option. Without a source container, an allowed token returns false from `has`, undefined from `resolveIfCreated`, and a missing-service error from `resolve`. Standalone `@nocobase/db` runners accept an owner-supplied resolver; the application allowlist is enforced by `app-server`, including direct `createAppMigrator` and `createAppSeeder` calls.

Use the callback's `query` and `builder` for transactional database work. Services resolved from the container are application-scoped and are not automatically rebound to the migration or seed transaction. Keep historical schema definitions self-contained rather than delegating them to evolving application services.

Database commands load the runtime and application modules lazily from `server/runtime` and `server/app` below the application root, preferring `.ts` over `.js` when both exist. Module execution errors propagate without falling back to another file.

      E9 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/openapi/describe.d.ts:46–58

      apiValidator 校验 param/query/json 等目标并声明进 API 文档，失败为 400 INVALID_INPUT。

      /**
 * Validate one part of the request — `param`, `query`, `json`, `header`, `form` or `cookie` — against a Standard Schema
 * such as a zod schema, and declare that schema in the API document. An invalid request is answered `400
 * INVALID_ARGUMENT` with reason `INVALID_INPUT`, domain `app`, and a field violation per issue before the handler
 * runs, exactly as `parseApiInput()` answers; the handler reads the parsed value from `context.req.valid(target)`.
 * Because of that, the document lists the 400 for invalid input on every route with a validator, without the route
 * declaring it.
 *
 * ```ts
 * router.post('/orders/:orderId/cancel', describeRoute({ ... }), apiValidator('param', OrderParams), apiValidator('json', CancelOrderInput), handler);
 * ```
 */
export declare function apiValidator<Schema extends StandardSchemaV1, Target extends keyof ValidationTargets, E extends Env = Env, P extends string = string>(target: Target, schema: Schema, options?: ApiValidatorOptions): MiddlewareHandler<E, P, ApiValidatorInput<Schema, Target>>;

      E10 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:9–14

      指引：/api 路由经 defineApiRoutes 贡献，每个路由都要 describeRoute 与 apiValidator。

      | `defineApiRoutes()`  | `/orders`        | `/api/orders`    | Application APIs the browser calls               |
| `defineRootRoutes()` | `/callbacks/pay` | `/callbacks/pay` | Webhooks, OAuth callbacks, protocol entry points |

Do not repeat `/api` in the path, and never write the deployment base path such as `/main` — both are added by the runtime.

Name paths, shape responses and errors, validate input and declare each route for the API document as [HTTP API design](http-api.md) describes. Every `/api` route carries a `describeRoute()` and validates its input with `apiValidator()`, both from `@nocobase/app-server/router`; a route without a declaration is a defect.

      E13 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/resource-types.js:117–154

      ResourceTypeRegistry 校验 catalog/record 类型、动作唯一与保留的 composite 类型；未声明 authorize 时默认 grantBacked()。

      /**
 * Every resource type. A catalog type has items and validates the item and
 * action; a record type has none, validates only the action and leaves the
 * record id to its own judgement.
 */
export class ResourceTypeRegistry {
    registered = new Map();
    constructor() {
        handlers.set(this, new Map());
    }
    add(definition) {
        const type = definition.type;
        if (!type)
            throw new TypeError('A resource type needs a name');
        if (type === RESERVED_TYPE && !addingReserved)
            throw new Error(`Resource type ${RESERVED_TYPE} is reserved for composite resources; use authz.compositeResources.define`);
        if (this.registered.has(type))
            throw new Error(`Resource type already registered: ${type}`);
        const actions = definition.actions?.map((action) => typeof action === 'string' ? { name: action } : action);
        const names = actions?.map((action) => action.name) ?? [];
        if (names.some((name) => !name) || new Set(names).size !== names.length)
            throw new TypeError(`Resource type ${type} needs unique, nonempty actions`);
        const items = definition.items;
        if (!items && !actions?.length)
            throw new TypeError(`Resource type ${type} needs items or declared actions`);
        const declared = actions?.map(({ name, title }) => title === undefined ? { name } : { name, title });
        if (items) {
            if (itemBindings.has(items))
                throw new Error(`Resource items already belong to a type: ${type}`);
            const binding = {
                type,
                ...(declared ? { actions: declared } : {}),
            };
            for (const item of itemEntries.get(items)?.values() ?? [])
                validateItem(item, binding);
            itemBindings.set(items, binding);
        }
        const typeAuthorize = definition.authorize ?? grantBacked();

      原文件 SHA-256：d77c0973a4cfa0b750f3799cfa31d3f313c8c87a80a284fdc6c42454bd740712

      authorizeRepository 要求单集合单范围的 composite action，并按 Repository 方法绑定读写动作。

      export function createCompositeRepositoryAuthorization(authz, options) {
    if (!options.repository || options.repository.includes('*'))
        throw new TypeError('Repository name must be non-empty and contain no wildcard');
    const repository = options.repository;
    const resource = options.resource.name;
    const bindings = new Map();
    for (const [method, action] of Object.entries(options.actions)) {
        if (!Object.hasOwn(operations, method) || typeof action !== 'string')
            throw new TypeError('Invalid Repository action binding');
        const definition = authz.compositeResources.getAction(resource, action);
        const scopes = definition?.dataScopes ?? [];
        const target = definition && scopes.length === 1
            ? dataScopeTarget(definition, scopes[0].key)
            : undefined;
        const collection = target?.type === 'database.collection' ? target.id : undefined;
        if (!definition ||
            !collection ||
            definition.grants.some((grant) => grant.resource.type !== 'database.collection' ||
                grant.resource.id !== collection) ||
            !definition.grants.some((grant) => grant.actions.some((entry) => entry.action === operations[method]))) {
            throw new TypeError(`Repository ${repository}:${method} requires a single-collection, single-scope composite action with the matching database operation`);
        }
        bindings.set(method, { action, collection });
    }
    const identity = authz.middleware();

      E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md:21–23

      指引：业务操作检查 composite 并绑定 conditions.database 策略，不得用 collection 级或内存过滤替代；页面用 page authz。

      Declare reusable collection permissions and business operations, defined as composite resources, in a feature module. In `server/providers/`, resolve `authorizationToken`, then register collections (`authz.database.collections.add`), composites (`authz.compositeResources.define`), record access (`authz.recordAccess.define`), settings items (`authz.settings.add`) and, when the feature owns a team or department model, inherited subjects (`authz.subjects.add`, built as [an organisation dimension](organization.md)); add workspace subsections with `authz.ui.sections.add` and list each composite or settings item in one with `authz.ui.place`. Pages are not registered on the server; the client routes list them. Read the authorization Skill’s bundled `references/code-and-seeds.md` before choosing initialization: code declares capabilities, provider boot registers them, and seeds initialize the agreed business configuration for fresh installations. Use authorized services or controlled data changes for existing installations. Seeded permission sets, selected scopes, rules and assignments remain editable in the backend; seeds do not make them code-owned or protected. App features own composite declarations and initial business configuration, not root/member protection or other platform permission configuration. Never rewrite administrator configuration at boot. Normal seeds have a restricted database context, not the running authorization service. Keep schema changes in migrations and initial assignments in idempotent seeds or controlled provisioning. Do not create a plugin package for an application-owned feature.

Every `server/routes/` endpoint installs authentication and authorization. A business operation checks `{ resource: { type: 'composite', id }, action }` with the request context's `authorize` once and binds each returned `conditions.database` policy through `repository.withPolicy`. Do not replace it with collection-level authorization or an in-memory filter. Use `authz.database.policyFor` for collection CRUD and `authz.database.authorizeRepository` for simple business Repository routes, mapping each method to its composite action. The authorization Skill documents these choices and their contracts.

      原文件 SHA-256：679bc55a250a456e0fa531f798d8e8423c8736dcdc032ac3f11a0d3deb298ee9

      E18 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:64–95

      文档给出的种子写入形状：id=key、encodeAuthorizationTitle、transaction:true、先查后插。

      For initial installation, a seed may write the documented persistence rows below after the owning plugins' migrations. Keep this adapter confined to installation data, use fluent values as its input, and test against the installed schema. Runtime routes must use the services instead. Keep one seed-data module per table.

```ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';
import { encodeAuthorizationTitle } from '@nocobase/authorization/core';
import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609190001_sales_jobs',
  transaction: true,
  async run({ query }) {
    const existing = await query
      .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .select('id')
      .where('key', '=', engineer.key)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    if (existing) return; // Preserve an administrator's existing definition.
    const now = new Date();
    await query
      .insertInto('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .values({
        id: engineer.key,
        key: engineer.key,
        title: encodeAuthorizationTitle(engineer.title),
        grants: JSON.stringify(engineer.grants),
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      })
      .execute();
  },
});
```

      E19 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/define.js:11–50

      defineSchedule 校验 key/title/cron/timezone/target 并冻结返回规范化定义。

      export function defineSchedule(definition) {
    if (!definition.key || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$/.test(definition.key))
        throw new Error('Schedule key must be a stable identifier.');
    if (!definition.title)
        throw new Error('Schedule title is required.');
    const parts = definition.schedule?.cron?.trim().split(/\s+/) ?? [];
    if (parts.length !== 5 && parts.length !== 6)
        throw new Error('Schedule cron must contain five or six fields.');
    const timezone = definition.schedule.timezone ?? 'UTC';
    try {
        CronExpressionParser.parse(definition.schedule.cron, {
            currentDate: new Date('2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
            tz: timezone,
        });
    }
    catch {
        throw new Error('Schedule cron or timezone is invalid.');
    }
    if (!definition.target?.type)
        throw new Error('Schedule target type is required.');
    assertNoSensitiveConfig(definition.target.config);
    if (definition.schedule.limit !== undefined &&
        (!Number.isInteger(definition.schedule.limit) ||
            definition.schedule.limit < 1))
        throw new Error('Schedule limit must be a positive integer.');
    if (definition.schedule.from &&
        definition.schedule.to &&
        definition.schedule.from > definition.schedule.to)
        throw new Error('Schedule from must not be after to.');
    const normalized = {
        ...definition,
        schedule: {
            ...definition.schedule,
            cron: definition.schedule.cron.trim(),
            timezone,
        },
    };
    const canonical = stableStringify(normalized);
    return freezeDeep({
        ...normalized,

      export interface ScheduleDefinition {
    readonly key: string;
    readonly title: string;
    readonly description?: string;
    readonly schedule: {

        readonly timezone?: string;
        readonly from?: Date;
        readonly to?: Date;
        readonly limit?: number;
    };
    readonly target: {
        readonly type: string;
        readonly config: JsonObject;
    };
}
export interface NormalizedScheduleDefinition extends Omit<ScheduleDefinition, 'schedule'> {
    readonly schedule: Required<Pick<ScheduleDefinition['schedule'], 'cron' | 'timezone'>> & Omit<ScheduleDefinition['schedule'], 'cron' | 'timezone'>;
    readonly definitionHash: string;
}
export declare function defineSchedule(definition: ScheduleDefinition): NormalizedScheduleDefinition;

      E23 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:41–49

      开发循环：在 Provider register/boot 中 resolve schedulerServiceToken 并 defineSchedule，且业务需自设幂等。

      ## Development Loop

1. Establish which tasks and execution records administrators need to see. Check Server/Client/CLI registration, page permissions, the application's `jobs` configuration (a `redis` adapter for more than one instance; `memory` serves one process) and `JobExecutorServiceProvider` composition, and target availability, then choose an application-owned target type or `workflow`.
2. Implement business logic and Providers in application source, and call `schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule(definition)` with an application-wide stable key from that Provider's `register()`/`boot()`.
3. Validate payload/input, timezone, idempotency, and the full asynchronous chain: submission, actual job execution, terminal notification, and recovery from persisted execution state. Obtain credentials through secure business Service configuration, never `target.config`.
4. Run application type checks, relevant tests, and build. Synchronize definitions and, in development, use an administrator account to find the task in the UI and track a real execution to its final state. Confirm the business result.
5. Report the schedule key, execution model, timezone, validation evidence, and unverified runtime boundaries. Use `--finalize` in production only when the complete manifest is visible.

Schedules are defined in code. There is no management API for creating or editing Cron definitions, but the UI and API support enabling and disabling tasks. Do not modify `schedule_definitions`, `schedule_occurrences`, or the schedule backend's rules (BullMQ job schedulers, or the memory adapter's state file) directly, and do not add jobs under Scheduler's `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler` scope.

      原文件 SHA-256：4399fba36c98661ac61c08026539960c74de06809e28d2686f844a8dce704eb9

      E24 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/overlay.md:120–149

      指引：useRouteOverlay 只能在 overlay 内部调用；页面只渲染 RouteDialog，需要 close 的部分拆成子组件。

      `useRouteOverlay()` returns `{ close, isClosing }`:

- `close(): Promise<void>`: closes the current overlay layer. Calling it again while a close is in progress returns the same Promise.
- `isClosing`: `true` from the `close()` call until navigation finishes (including the wait for `beforeClose`); use it to disable buttons such as "Cancel".

**Call it only in a component inside the overlay**, including a component passed as `footer`. The context comes from `RouteDialog`/`RouteDrawer`, and the page component that renders the overlay is outside it:

```tsx
// Wrong: NewProjectPage renders the RouteDialog, so it is itself outside that RouteDialog.
export default function NewProjectPage(): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  // Throws when there is no overlay outside; when another overlay is outside, it gets that outer close and closes the wrong layer.
  const { close } = useRouteOverlay();
  return (
    <RouteDialog
      title={t('projects.create.title')}
      footer={
        <Button variant='outline' onClick={() => void close()}>
          {t('actions.cancel')}
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {/* … */}
    </RouteDialog>
  );
}
```

- With no overlay outside, it throws `useRouteOverlay must be used inside RouteDialog or RouteDrawer`. When nested, it does not throw; instead it silently gets the parent overlay's `close` and closes the wrong layer. Context is looked up through the parent-child relationships of React components, regardless of the source file or the DOM location a portal renders into.
- The right way: the page component only renders `RouteDialog`; split the parts that need to close into separate components (for example, `NewProjectBody` and `NewProjectFooter`) and render them as JSX (`<NewProjectFooter />`, not the function call `NewProjectFooter()`; otherwise the hook does not run inside the overlay).

      原文件 SHA-256：6ac2ce265b93a429751fc228699dfae331817a42a0a1bea89dffa588a0a1b22d

      E25 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/child-routes.md:10–16

      指引：父页面自放 Outlet；独立子页面返回 RouteChildPage，其余用 RouteDialog/RouteDrawer 覆盖；子路由继承父 authz。

      - A child route's `path` is relative to its parent and is appended to the parent's path. A leading `/` is stripped before joining, so `new` and `/new` behave the same; this handbook writes the form without `/`.
- **The parent page must place `<Outlet />` itself**, where the child content should appear. Pages do not insert an Outlet automatically, and neither do `RouteChildPage`, `RouteDialog` or `RouteDrawer`; only a plain navigation group gets its Outlet from the route renderer.
- **A child route that is a page of its own returns `RouteChildPage`.** Only tab content renders inline. A child route with a `PageContainer` of its own — a record's page, a form too long for a dialog, an import page — wraps it in `RouteChildPage` ([section 5](#5-covering-child-pages-routechildpage)). Returned bare, it renders at the parent's `Outlet`, below everything the parent shows, and the user has to scroll past the parent page to find it. A page that returns a bare `PageContainer` belongs on a top-level route instead.
- Child routes inherit the entry route's `auth` and cannot change it, and follow the `authz` inheritance rules in [section 4 of `page.md`](page.md#4-authz-page-authorization): a child page renders only after the parent page's check passes.
- Link to a child route with a relative path and keep the query string ([section 2.2 of `overlay.md`](overlay.md#22-place-the-outlet-in-the-parent-page)). A relative path resolves against the route that renders the link, not the URL on screen ([section 2 of `page.md`](page.md#2-the-page-component)).
- Route declarations have no `index` field. Do not invent an index route, and do not register a child route at the parent's own path; when "opening the parent URL shows a particular child page" is needed, use the redirect in [section 4](#4-page-tabs).
- Do not change the shell, the route renderer or the ServiceProvider to add a menu entry; menu entries come only from `navigation` on routes ([section 6 of `page.md`](page.md#6-menus)).

      const seed = defineSeed({
  name: '202609150002_office_role_permission_sets',
  async run({ query }) {
    const now = new Date();

    for (const set of OFFICE_ROLE_PERMISSION_SETS) {
      const existing = await query
        .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSets')

      E30 · app/client/components/use-route-overlay.ts:20–44

      被修改的模板组件：useRouteOverlay 在 overlay 外抛错，并新增 useRouteOverlayRef 以承接页面级异步关闭。

      /**
 * Closes the enclosing `RouteDialog` or `RouteDrawer`. Call it from a component rendered inside the overlay — its
 * body or its footer — never from the page that returns the overlay, which sits outside the provider.
 */
export function useRouteOverlay(): RouteOverlayContextValue {
  const value = useContext(RouteOverlayContext);
  if (!value) {
    throw new Error(
      'useRouteOverlay must be used inside RouteDialog or RouteDrawer',
    );
  }
  return value;
}

/**
 * Captures the controls of the overlay a page renders.
 *
 * The page component that returns `RouteDialog`/`RouteDrawer` sits outside the overlay's context, so it cannot call
 * `useRouteOverlay()` directly. It renders `<RouteOverlayBridge controlsRef={ref} />` among the overlay's children;
 * the bridge captures the controls, and the page closes the overlay through `ref.current?.close()` after an async
 * save, when no descendant is left to do it.
 */
export function useRouteOverlayRef(): RefObject<RouteOverlayContextValue | null> {
  return useRef<RouteOverlayContextValue | null>(null);
}

      E31 · app/client/components/route-overlay-bridge.tsx:1–29

      应用新增 RouteOverlayBridge：渲染为空，把 overlay controls 抄进 ref，供页面保存后关闭。

      import { useLayoutEffect, type ReactElement, type RefObject } from 'react';

import {
  useRouteOverlay,
  type RouteOverlayContextValue,
} from './use-route-overlay.js';

export interface RouteOverlayBridgeProps {
  readonly controlsRef: RefObject<RouteOverlayContextValue | null>;
}

/**
 * Renders nothing and copies the enclosing overlay's controls into `controlsRef`.
 *
 * It must be rendered as a child of `RouteDialog`/`RouteDrawer`. It exists so the page component that returns the
 * overlay can close it after a save, which is the one place that has to happen from outside a descendant.
 */
export function RouteOverlayBridge({
  controlsRef,
}: RouteOverlayBridgeProps): ReactElement | null {
  const controls = useRouteOverlay();
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    controlsRef.current = controls;
    return () => {
      controlsRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [controls, controlsRef]);
  return null;
}

      E33 · app/server/office/reporting.ts:130–153

      应用以 idempotencyKey=office-low-stock-<date> 调 notification.send，同一天重试不重复通知。

        /**
   * Sends the low-stock reminder to whoever manages the warehouse. The
   * scheduler owns *when*; this owns *what* and *to whom*.
   *
   * The idempotency key is the day, so a retried job on the same day does not
   * notify the same person twice, while tomorrow's run is a new send.
   */
  async sendLowStockReminder(): Promise<LowStockReminderResult> {
    const reminder = await this.buildLowStockReminder();
    const { notification, reminderRecipients } = this.context;
    if (reminder.count === 0 || !notification || !reminderRecipients) {
      return { ...reminder, sentTo: [] };
    }
    const recipients = await reminderRecipients();
    if (recipients.length === 0) return { ...reminder, sentTo: [] };
    await notification.send({
      idempotencyKey: `office-low-stock-${codeDate()}`,
      source: { type: 'office.low-stock-reminder' },
      messages: {
        inbox: {
          to: [...recipients],
          title: reminder.title,
          body: reminder.body,
          target: { type: 'route', path: '/office/inventory' },

      E34 · app/server/office/queries.ts:101–155

      moveStock 先查幂等流水提前返回，再以 stockQuantity=balanceBefore 条件更新，更新数 0 时抛 conflict。

       * A caller inside a transaction rolls everything back on any of these.
 */
export async function moveStock(
  query: QueryAdapter,
  input: MoveStockInput,
): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await query
    .selectFrom(TABLE.stockMovements)
    .select([`${TABLE.stockMovements}.id`])
    .where(`${TABLE.stockMovements}.supplyId`, '=', input.supplyId)
    .where(`${TABLE.stockMovements}.sourceType`, '=', input.sourceType)
    .where(`${TABLE.stockMovements}.sourceId`, '=', input.sourceId)
    .executeTakeFirst();
  if (existing) return;

  const supply = await query
    .selectFrom(TABLE.supplies)
    .select([
      `${TABLE.supplies}.id`,
      `${TABLE.supplies}.name`,
      `${TABLE.supplies}.stockQuantity`,
    ])
    .where(`${TABLE.supplies}.id`, '=', input.supplyId)
    .executeTakeFirst();
  if (!supply) {
    throw failedPrecondition(`Supply ${input.supplyId} no longer exists`, {
      supplyId: input.supplyId,
    });
  }
  const balanceBefore = toInteger(supply.stockQuantity);
  const balanceAfter = balanceBefore + input.delta;
  if (balanceAfter < 0) {
    throw failedPrecondition(
      `Insufficient stock for ${toText(supply.name) ?? `supply ${input.supplyId}`}: ${balanceBefore} available, ${Math.abs(input.delta)} requested`,
      {
        supplyId: input.supplyId,
        available: balanceBefore,
        requested: Math.abs(input.delta),
        shortages: true,
      },
    );
  }

  const updated = await query
    .updateTable(TABLE.supplies)
    .set({ stockQuantity: balanceAfter, updatedAt: new Date() })
    .where(`${TABLE.supplies}.id`, '=', input.supplyId)
    .where(`${TABLE.supplies}.stockQuantity`, '=', balanceBefore)
    .execute();
  if (!toInteger(updated.updatedCount)) {
    throw conflict('The stock balance changed while it was being updated', {
      supplyId: input.supplyId,
    });
  }

      本轮未完成

      本轮不是完成交付。请查看本轮验收记录和问题记录。

      What was delivered

      业务场景与交付说明

      采购到入库闭环

      新建采购单页脚自动计算总额(52.50/105.00/250.00)，提交后由主管批准；批准前无收货按钮，批准后可收货入库，库存随收货增加，并生成带下载文件名的 HTML 打印件。

      角色权限隔离

      员工导航无供应商，仅见本人领用单，访问他人单据返回 not found，设置页不可用且详情无出库按钮；主管可见并批准全部采购单；库管可见全部并可办理领用出库、采购收货与手工调整。

      库存规则与数据能力

      库存不足的领用单整单拦截、状态与库存不变；重复点击出库只产生一条扣减流水。CSV 导入成功新增用品并按编码解析供应商，非法行返回行级错误且不写库；库存导出为带文件名的 CSV。

      看板与低库存提醒

      首页展示库存数、库存金额、低库存数与待收货数及低库存清单，批准未收货采购单后待收货由0变1；低库存提醒列出5项并成功发送库管，调度任务为每天08:00 Asia/Shanghai。

      01管理员创建用品与供应商并编辑用品
      →
      02新建采购单自动计算总额并提交审批
      →
      03主管批准采购单
      →
      04库管收货入库，库存增加
      →
      05员工提交领用申请
      →
      06库管出库扣减库存，库存不足整单拦截
      →
      07导入用品清单与导出当前库存
      →
      08首页看板与低库存每日提醒

      原始验收要求

      我是行政负责人，需要一个单仓库的办公用品采购和领用系统。管理用品、供应商、采购单和领用单，完成采购申请、主管批准、收货入库和员工领用。采购单自动计算总额，可以下载打印，批准后才允许收货入库。 员工只查看自己的申请，库管办理出入库，主管查看全部。库存不足时整张领用单不能出库，重复点击不能重复扣库存；低于安全库存时每天提醒库管。支持导入用品清单、导出当前库存，首页展示库存情况、待收货采购单和低库存用品。

      实际操作

      1. 以 admin(nocobase/admin123) 登录 http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/ 进入业务界面
      2. 用品：新建 QA验收用品(QA-ACC-001, 库存50, 安全库存10, 单价9.90, 供应商晨光文具供应)并在列表确认
      3. 用品编辑：打开编辑表单确认原值回填(name/price9.90/safety10/当前余额50只读)，改为 QA验收用品-改、10.50、安全库存12 并保存，列表与值已持久
      4. 供应商：新建 QA测试供应商(SUP-QA-01)并在列表确认
      5. 采购单：新建 PO-20261007-0001(晨光文具供应, QA-ACC-001 x5 @10.50)，页脚 Total 自动为 52.50；Draft→Submit for approval→Pending approval→Approve→Approved
      6. 收货入库：批准前详情无 Receive 按钮，批准后出现 Receive into stock；点击后状态 Received，库存 50→55
      7. 打印：Print 链接 href=/main/api/office/purchase-orders/1/document target=_blank；fetch 返回 200 text/html，Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="PO-20261007-0001.html"
      8. 领用单不足拦截：SR-20261007-0001 数量100>库存55，详情提示 Not enough stock，Issue 失败 Insufficient stock for QA验收用品-改: 55 available, 100 requested，状态仍 Pending approval，库存不变
      9. 领用出库：SR-20261007-0002 数量2，Issue 成功，状态 Issued，库存 55→53；再次点击 Issue 未重复扣减
      10. 角色：admin 在 用户管理 新建 qa_employee(员工小王/Employee)、qa_supervisor(主管李/Supervisor)、qa_keeper(库管张/Warehouse keeper)
      11. 员工：退出并重新加载后登录 qa_employee，导航无 Suppliers；领用列表仅见自己的 SR-20261007-0003，直接打开 /office/requisitions/1 报 Requisition 1 was not found；/settings/users 显示 No settings available；自建领用单后详情只有 Cancel 无 Issue
      12. 主管：登录 qa_supervisor，领用列表与采购单列表可见全部数据；对 PO-20261007-0003 执行 Approve 成功，Approved by 主管李
      13. 库管：登录 qa_keeper，领用列表可见全部，对 SR-20261007-0003 执行 Issue 成功(Issued by 库管张，库存53→52)；对已批准 PO-20261007-0002 执行 Receive into stock 成功，库存53→63；库存页 Adjust stock +1 成功(existing 52→53, operator 库管张)
      14. 导入：用品 Import 页粘贴 CSV(name,sku,...,supplierCode) 导入 QA导入用品(QA-IMP-001,20,5,3.50,SUP-QA-01) 成功 Imported 1 and updated 0，列表出现且供应商按编码解析为 QA测试供应商；非法行(缺 sku/数字非法)返回 0 created, 0 updated, 1 skipped 并提示 Row 1: "sku" is required，列表未写入脏数据
      15. 导出：库存页 Export 链接 /main/api/office/inventory/export 返回 200 text/csv，Content-Disposition attachment; filename="inventory-20261007.csv"，含库存52与低于安全库存标记
      16. 提醒：库存页 Low-stock reminder 列出5项低于安全库存，Send now 返回 Reminder sent to 1 recipient(s).；设置 Automation→Scheduled tasks 存在 办公用品低库存提醒/Office low-stock reminder，目标 office.low-stock-reminder，At 08:00 AM Asia/Shanghai
      17. 首页：/main 看板显示 Supplies 12 / Inventory value 5,025.00 / Low stock 5 / Awaiting receipt，列表 Supplies below safety stock 与 Approved orders awaiting receipt；批准 PO-20261007-0003 后 Awaiting receipt 由 0 变为 1 并列出该单

      观察结果

      1. 用品创建与编辑均真实提交并在列表/详情体现，编辑表单原值正确回填(当前余额50只读显示 Current balance: 50)
      2. 采购单总额自动计算：5x10.50=52.50、10x10.50=105.00、100x2.50=250.00 均在页脚 Total 正确显示
      3. 收货按钮仅在 Approved 后出现，未批准(Draft/Pending)时详情无 Receive into stock
      4. 打印接口返回 HTML 附件且带下载文件名，符合可下载打印要求
      5. 库存不足时整张领用单被拦截，状态与库存均不变化；重复点击 Issue 仅产生一条 -2 库存流水，余额只扣一次
      6. 员工仅见自己的 SR-20261007-0003，访问他人领用单返回 not found，设置页不可用，领用详情无 Issue；主管可见全部领用单/采购单并可批准；库管可见全部并可办理领用出库、采购收货与手工调整
      7. CSV 导入成功新增用品并解析供应商编码；非法行给出精确行级错误且未写库；导出为带文件名的 CSV 附件并含当前库存
      8. 低库存提醒弹窗列出5项并成功发送给库管，调度任务显示每天 08:00 (Asia/Shanghai) 执行 office.low-stock-reminder
      9. 首页看板正确展示库存概览、低库存用品与待收货采购单，并在产生已批准未收货采购单后由 0 更新为 1
      10. 全流程未出现 Something went wrong、空白页、未处理异常或非预期 4xx/5xx
